<!-- page 1 -->
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
Tuesday, January 12, 2016 
 
PRESENT: Robert K. Black Jr., Supervisor; Jesse Thompson, Supervisor; Steve Williams, Supervisor; Jason E. 
Whiting, Vice-Chairman; and Dawnafe Whitesinger, Chairwoman were present.  
9:09 a.m.  Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation led by Supervisor Black. 
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  
a. Election of Cha irman and Vice -Chairman for 2016 : Supervisor Williams made a motion  to elect  
Supervisor Whiting to serve as Chair man and Supervisor Thompson to serve as Vice Chairman  for 
2016; motion seconded by Supervisor Black. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Presentation of a Plaque of Appreciation for 2015 Service to Chair woman Dawnafe Whitesinger : 
Chairman Whiting recognized Supervisor Whitesinger for her service as Chairwoman during the 2015 
year.  Appreciation was expressed to Supervisor Whitesinger from the Board members for her leadership 
as chairwoman. Supervisor Whitesinger stated she is grateful for the opportunity to serve with the Board 
and represent Navajo County.  
      CALL TO THE PUBLIC: Terry Hill, Commander of VFW Post 9907 in Show Low expressed his appreciation 
to Supervisor Whitesinger for her service as c hairwoman. He indicated the auxiliary provided food boxes for 
63 families, toys for 87 children, and a total of 441 people received a week’s worth of groceries. Commander Hill 
talked of an  organization called “Honor F lights” which t akes World War II Veterans to see the Washington 
memorials, the Arlington National Cemetery and the World War II Memorial . He stated the VFW will hold a 
Spaghetti Dinner  fundraiser on February 20 th for this event, to sponsor a 98 year old WWII veteran of the 
Women’s Army Auxiliary Corp. He reported Sadee Reidhead placed 4h in the state’s Veterans of Foreign Wars 
Patriot Pen essay contest. He indicated she received a cash award of $350.00. 
CONSENT AGENDA: a) Minutes: December 8, 2015 and December 29, 2015 Special Meeting b) Warrant & 
Voucher Report over $1000 December 2015 c) Clerk of Superior Court Report November 2015 d) Juvenile 
Probation Report November 2015 e) Constable Reports: Show Low Precinct #5, Nove mber & December and 
Pinetop-Lakeside Precinct #6, November 2015 f) Justice Court Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1, November; 
Winslow Precinct #2, November; Snowflake Precinct #3, November; Kayenta Precinct #4, November; Show Low 
Precinct #5, November; and Pin etop-Lakeside Precinct #6, November 2015 g) Independent Contractor 
Agreement, Homero Vela, for professional services for Navajo County Public Works, Flood Control, Health 
District and Intergovernmental Relations with the Navajo Nation, the White Mountain A pache Tribe and Hopi 
Tribe h) Contract for Public Defense Services performed by Dirk LeGate for cases assigned out of Superior Court 
Division II from January 4, 2016 – June 30, 2016 i) Conduct Board Member Elections for the City of Winslow 
on March 8, 2016 j) Contracts signed by the County Manager per Board of Supervisors Resolution: Modification 
of Grant or Agreement with the USDA Forest Service k) Approval of all properties sold as auctioned on the back 
tax land on-line auction and approval to allow the Clerk of the Board to sell the unsold properties over the counter 
for the amount due l) Sympathy  Letter to Leonard Haskie family:  Supervisor Williams made a motion to 
authorize the Chairman to sign  the items in the consent agenda as presented;  motion seconded by Supervisor 
Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.

<!-- page 2 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 1-12-2016 
    Page 2 of 4 
 
      BOARD OF SUPERVISORS: Consideration and possible approval of a Proclamation declaring January 27th 
thru January 29th, 2016 as Hashknife Pony Express Week : Chairman Whiting read the proclamation. 
Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to approve the Proclamation declaring January 27th thru January 29th, 
2016 as Hashknife Pony Express Week; motion seconded by Supervisor Black. Vote unanimous approving the 
motion. Mark Reynolds, Hashknife Posse Captain reported on the dates and times of the Hashknife Posse Week 
events. 
 
      HUMAN RESOURCES: Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions: Laura Carell reviewed the 
38 item personnel action report. Supervisor Williams made a motion to approve the personnel action report as 
presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Mr. Jayne gave 
an update on the Winslow Complex regarding a fire yesterday morning in the probation wing of the complex. He 
indicated that Stephen Pauken, Winslow City Manager and the Winslow Fire Department are working to find the 
cause of the fire. He stated a restoration crew will repair the facility. 
 
      NAVAJO COUNTY PUBLIC HEALTH SERVICES DISTRICT Board of Dir ectors Session: CONSENT 
AGENDA: Supervisor Thompson made a motion to enter into the Navajo County Public Health Services 
District Board of Directors Session; motion seconded by Supervisor Williams. Vote unanimous approving the 
motion.  
a. Renewal of Professional Services Agreement contract with Kristi Wahl, RN, for Public Health Nursing 
services through the HIV, STD, Immunization, TB and Family Planning grants, as well as billable nursing 
service programs; effective January 1, 2016 to December 31, 2016 in the amount of $35 per hour 
b. Professional Services Agreement contract with Debra Smalley, CNM, for Nurse Practitioner services 
through the Title V Family Planning Grant, and Family Planning Insured Programs; effective January 1, 
2016 to December 31, 2016 in the amount of $2,000 per month 
c. Renewal of Professional Services Agreement contract with Rebecca Sowers, RN, for Public Health 
Nursing services through the HIV, STD, Immunization, TB and Family Planning grants, as well as billable 
nursing service programs; effective January 1, 2016 to December 31, 2016 in the amount of $35 per hour 
d. Renewal of Professional Services Agreement contract with Mauriah Walker, RN, for Public Health 
Nursing services through the HIV, STD, Immunization, TB and Family Planning grants, as well as billable 
nursing service programs; effective January 1, 2016 to December 31, 2016 in the amount of $35 per hour 
e. Professional Services contract with Tamra Cannon, RDH, for Dental Hygiene services through the Dental 
Sealant Program effective January 1, 2016 to December 31, 2016 in the amount of $45.00 per hour 
f. Amendment #2 of Contract #ADHS15 -094982 with the Arizona Department of Health Services for the 
Healthy People Healthy Communities grant in the amount of $411,701.50 
            Mary Herring, Health Director discussed the item. Supervisor Thompson made a motion to approve the consent 
agenda as presented; motion seconded by  Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
Supervisor Black made a motion to return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote 
unanimous approving the motion.  
WHITE MOUNTAIN LAKE S COUNTY RECREATION IMPROVEMENT DISTRICT: Board of 
Directors Session:  Supervisor Williams  made a motion to ent er into the White Mountain Lake s County 
Recreation Improvement District Board of Directors Session; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote 
unanimous approving the motion . Consideration and possible appointment of White Mountain Lakes County 
Recreation Improvement District Advisory Committee Members: Jerry McGraw addressed the Board regarding 
the votes for the three open positions on the White Mountain Lakes County Recreation Improvement District 
Advisory Committee. Mr. McGraw reported that they have voted on the three open seats and an alternate; David 
Adams- 4 votes, Bill Kaiser- 4 votes, Marc Silliman- 3 votes and Carl Erdmann- 2 votes He asked the Board to

<!-- page 3 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 1-12-2016 
    Page 3 of 4 
 
seat the advisory committee members as recommended. Supervisor Williams made a motion to approve: David 
Adams, Bill Kaiser, and Marc Silliman , with Carl Erdman as the alternate; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Mr. McGraw stated they will be back for the  January 26th 
Board of Supervisor meeting regarding a bylaw change. At 9:55 a.m. Brad Carlyon, County Attorney asked the 
Board to modify the motion for the alternate  indicating that their bylaws do not support seating an alternate . 
Supervisor Williams made a motion to approve the appointment of White Mountain Lakes County Recreation 
Improvement District Advisory Committee Members, with no alternate being appointed ; motion seconded by  
Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Supervisor Black made a motion to return to 
regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor Williams. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
      COUNTY ATTORNEY: Consideration and acceptance of FY16 Police Data Sharing Grant , Number NCP-16-
002: Lynda Young addressed the board regarding the FY 16 Police Data Sharing Grant : Brad Carlyon, County 
Attorney spoke about the Spillman system. He indicated the funds from the ACJC Police Data Sharing, will fund 
the data sharing software bridge between the Navajo County Attorney’s Office and the Navajo County Sheriff’s 
Office. He indicated that with these funds, both offices will see an increase in communication, productivity and 
information sharing between both agencies, which will save money and time. Supervisor Whitesinger made a 
motion to approve the FY16 Police Data Sharing Grant, Number NCP-16-002; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Williams. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
EMERGENCY MANAGEMENT:  
a. Consideration and possible approval of a Pre -Disaster Mitigation Award: Catrina Jenkins addressed the 
Board regarding a Pre-Disaster Mitigation grant in the amount of $30,000. Supervisor Thompson made 
a motion to approve of a Pre-Disaster Mitigation Award; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. 
Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of one Homeland Security Grant Award: Catrina Jenkins addressed 
the Board regarding a Homeland Security Grant Award in the amount of $5,500.00 from reallocated funds. 
Supervisor Thompson  made a motion  to approve of one Homeland Security Grant Award ; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Williams. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
FINANCE: County Finance Update : James Menlove presented the  update. James Jayne introduced Bryan 
Layton, Assistant County Manager. Mr. Layton addressed the Board. The Board members each welcomed Mr. 
Layton to the Navajo County team. 
      NAVAJO COUNTY FLOOD CONTROL DISTRICT: Board of Directors Session: Supervisor Thompson 
made a motion  to enter into the Navajo County Flood Control District Board of Directors Session ; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Williams. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Consideration and possible approval 
of the purchase of Assessor’s Parcel Number 206 -28-043 for $25,000 to eliminate future construction in the 
floodway: Bill Bess  addressed the Board . Brad Carlyon t hanked Brandt Clark for his work on this item . 
Supervisor Williams made a motion  to approve the  purchase of Assessor’s Parcel Number 206 -28-043 for 
$25,000 to eliminate future construction in the floodway ; motion seconded by Supervisor Black. Vote 
unanimous approving the motion. Supervisor Williams made a motion  to return to regular session; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
      BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  Presentation to Assistant County Manager, Homero Vela for his s ervice to 
Navajo County: Melissa Buckley  presented a tribute video for Homero Vela , who has served the citizens of 
Navajo County for eleven years . Mr. Vela received many heartfelt words of appreciation and gifts . His well -
wishers included the Navajo County Board of Supervisors, James Jayne, Elected Officials, Department Directors,

<!-- page 4 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 1-12-2016 
    Page 4 of 4 
 
Eric Oscarson, Alberto Peshlakai, Ryan Taylor, Bryan Kenney with the United States Army Corp of Engineers, 
Winslow City Manager Stephen Pauken, Daryl Bradley with the Navajo Nation Department of Transportation, 
former supervisor J.R. DeSpain, former supervisor Percy Deal, Michael Lomayaktewa with the Hopi Department 
of Transportation , and former supervisor and Navajo Nation Vic e-President Jonathan M. Nez. Homero Vela 
addressed the Board and spoke about the partnerships and friendships that he has enjoyed while working at Navajo 
County and within the communities. He stated he was fortunate to be a part of these partnerships that are pursuing 
excellence for our community.  
      COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE:  Report from Board members, County Attorney and County Manager : 
Supervisor Whitesinger gave a brief update from District V and spoke of attending the Opening Day Ceremonies 
of the 52nd Legislature. Supervisor Black thanked the Public Works staff  and Mr. Vela  for their work  in his 
district. He reported on the Opening Day Legislative Session. Supervisor Williams spoke about the Speaker of 
the House, David Gowan’s visit and a meeting with Senator Sylvia Allen. Supervisor Thompson stated that he 
will attend the Tribal Legislative D ay, which will include 22 t ribes. James  Jayne reported on the  December 
newsletter from Emergency M anagement. Brad Carlyon , County Attorney  indicated that he is sharing the 
caseloads in his office, he is handling North County, Winslow Justice Court, and indicated he has three jury trials 
set for next week. Chairman Whiting spoke about the Legislative Opening Session and asked the Board to reach 
out to our legislators. He spoke about the Apache Railway. He presented an appreciation plaque to Navajo County 
from the Navajo County Fair Board.  
ADJOURN: At 11:33 p.m. Supervisor Williams made a motion to adjourn; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Black. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
 
APPROVED:       DATED: January 26, 2016 
 
Jason E. Whiting, Chairman 
Navajo County Board of Supervisors 
 
 
 
ATTEST: 
 
Melissa W. Buckley, Clerk 
Navajo County Board of Supervisors
